    BUSINESS OVERVIEW                                                     業務回顧
    The trade and tariff disputes between the United States and China     中美之間的貿易關稅爭端令全球宏觀經濟整體
    caused the overall slowdown of global macro-economy. In the           放緩。面對挑戰及困難，本集團憑藉獨特的客
    face of challenges and difficulties, the Group’s turnover growth      戶群及明智的業務策略，使年內的營業額增長
    during the year has not been greatly affected, thanks to its unique   不受較大影響。
    customer profile and sensible business strategies.

    As a reputable supplier of customised wiring components, the          作為知名的定制電線部件供應商，本公司堅持
    Company has given unremitting efforts in providing high-quality       不懈地為其客戶提供高質的產品及服務。於回
    products and services for its customers. During the Review Year,      顧年度內，本集團繼續向產品開發投放資源，
    the Group has continued to invest resources in product                並繼續進一步提高自身研發能力。本集團已推
    development and further enhance its R&D capabilities. It has          出更多 5G 下一代產品，以形成具有多元化產品
    launched more 5G next-generation products to form a product           及解決方案的產品組合。董事相信，持續創新
    mix of diversified products and solutions. The Directors believe      及出色的產品組合將提升本集團的整體競爭
    that the continuous innovation and outstanding product mix will       力，使其在同業對手中脫穎而出並有賴於擴大
    elevate the Group’s overall competitiveness, set it apart from the    本集團的市場份額。
    peers and leverage the expansion of the Group’s market share.

     INTRODUCTION                                                          引言
     The Board and the management of the Company are committed             本公司董事會及管理層致力於維持良好的企業
     to the maintenance of good corporate governance practices and         管治常規及程序。本公司相信，良好的企業管
     procedures. The Company believes that good corporate                  治為有效管理、健康的企業文化、成功的業務
     governance provides a framework that is essential for effective       增長及提升股東價值提供必要的框架。本公司
     management, healthy corporate culture, successful business            企業管治守則強調優質的董事會、完善的內部
     growth and enhancing shareholders’ value. The corporate               控制、透明及對全體股東負責。
     governance principles of the Company emphasise a quality Board,
     sound internal controls, and transparency and accountability to all
     shareholders.

     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICE                                         企業管治常規
     The Company acknowledges the need and importance of                   本公司確信企業管治是為股東創造價值之必要
     corporate governance as one of the key elements in creating           及重要元素之一，而本公司亦致力達至高水平
     shareholders’ value. The Company is also committed to achieving       之企業管治，以保障及提升全體股東利益，提
     high standard of corporate governance that can protect and            高企業價值與本公司之問責性。就企業管治目
     promote the interests of all Shareholders and to enhance              的而言，本公司已採納上市規則附錄十四所載
     corporate value and accountability of the Company. For corporate      的企業管治守則（「企管守則」）。截至二零一九
     governance purpose, the Company has adopted the Corporate             年三月三十一日止年度，就董事會所深知，本
     Governance Code (the “CG Code”) set out in Appendix 14 of the         公司一直遵守企管守則所載的所有適用守則條
     Listing Rules. During the year ended 31 March 2019, to the best       文。
     knowledge of the Board, the Company had complied with all the
     applicable code provisions set out in the CG Code.

     MODEL CODE FOR SECURITIES TRANSACTIONS                                董事進行證券交易之標準守則
     BY DIRECTORS
     The Company has adopted the Model Code as the code of                 本公司已採納標準守則作為有關董事進行證券
     conduct regarding securities transactions by directors. Having        交易之操守準則。經作出具體查詢後，全體董
     made specific enquiry, all Directors have fully complied with the     事已於整個期間全面遵守標準守則所載所須標
     required standards set out in the Model Code and there was no         準且概無出現不合規事件。
     event of non-compliance throughout the period.

DIRECTORS’ RESPONSIBILITIES AND DELEGATION                           董事職責及授權
The Board is responsible for leading and controlling the business    董事會負責領導及控制本集團的業務經營。其
operations of the Group. It formulates strategic directions,         制定策略方向、監督營運及監控本集團的財務
oversees the operations and monitors the financial performance of    表現。管理層獲董事會指派權力及授權進行本
the Group. The management is delegated power and authority           集團的日常管理及經營。管理層就本公司的整
given by the Board for the day-to-day management and                 體營運對董事會負責。根據職權範圍，董事會

           having a diverse Board to enhance the quality of its                    公司的表現質素裨益良多。
           performance.

           Board appointments will be based on merits, and candidates              才為原則，並在考慮人選時以客觀條件充

     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                   風險管理及內部監控
     The Directors acknowledge that they have overall responsibility for    董事明白彼等有全盤責任監督本公司內部監
     overseeing the Company’s internal control, financial control and       控、財務監控及風險管理系統，並須持續監察
     risk management system and shall monitor its effectiveness on an       其有效性。董事會至少每年對風險管理及內部
     ongoing basis. A review of the effectiveness of the risk               監控系統的有效性進行審閱。
     management and internal control systems has been conducted by
     the Board at least annually.

     Aimed at providing reasonable assurance against material errors,       本 公 司 著 力 提 供 合 理 保 證，避 免 出 現 重 大 錯
     losses or fraud, the Company has established a risk management         誤、損失或造假，為此已成立風險管理程序，
     procedures which comprised the following steps:                        包括以下步驟：

     —    Identify risks: Identify major and significant risks that could   —   識別風險：識別可能影響達成本集團目標
          affect the achievement of goals of the Group;                         之主要及重大風險；

          according to its likely impact and the likelihood of                  出現之可能性作出評估及評核；
          occurrence;

     —    Risk mitigation: Develop effective control activities to          —   紓緩風險：策劃有效的監管活動，務求紓
          mitigate the risks.                                                   緩風險。

     Risk identification and assessment is performed or updated             每年進行或更新風險識別及評估，風險評估、
     annually, and the results of risk assessment, evaluation and           評核的結果及各功能或營運之紓緩措施會詳細

     Risk Registry to communicate to the Board and Management for           閱。
     reviews.

     failure to achieve business objectives, and can only provide           險，僅可就重大失實聲明或損失提供合理而非
     reasonable and not absolute assurance against material                 絕對保證。

     management functions has been carried out by an independent            務、營運及合規監控及風險管理職能）進行審

extensively, and make substantive analysis more comprehensively.        加 強 重 要 性、 量 化、 平 衡 及 一 致 性 的 匯 報 原

Health and Safety                                                         健康與安全
The Hong Kong headquarters of the Group has been equipped                 本集團香港總部已配備消防設施（例如滅火器、
with fire-fighting facilities, e.g. fire extinguishers, smoke detectors   煙霧警報器等）並參與香港科學園定期舉辦的
and etc., and participated in the fire drill organised by the Hong        消防演習。
Kong Science Park regularly.

The production facilities in Huizhou engage in manufacturing              惠州生產設施從事生產電線裝配產品。本集團
cable assembly products. The Group strongly believes that                 堅信，維持一個安全與穩定的生產環境是本集
ensuring stable and safe production is the most important social          團對其持份者最重要的企業社會責任。因此安
responsibility to its stakeholders. Therefore, the Group has always       全與穩定的生產環境是其企業管理的重要一
regarded ensuring safe and stable production as one of the                環。本集團已建立了全面的職業健康安全管理
priorities in its corporate management. Sound management                  體系，並嚴格遵守中國職業健康與安全的相關
systems have been established in occupational health and safety in        法規。此外，惠州生產設施定期舉行消防演習。
strict compliance with the regulations on occupational health and
safety in the PRC. Moreover, fire drills are organised by the
production facilities in Huizhou on a regular basis.

Daily operations are inspected by relevant department assigned by         本集團已指定相關部門就已建立的風險評估計
the Group, against the established risk assessment program that           劃 進 行 日 常 營 運 檢 查，包 括 一 系 列 連 續 的 步
consists of a number of sequential steps such as risk identification,     驟， 如 基 於 現 有 控 制 及 建 議 的 風 險 識 別、 分
analysis, evaluation, treatment, monitoring and reviewing based           析、評估、處理、監督及檢討等，以減少被視
on the existing controls and recommendations to reduce those              為不可接受限度內的風險。任何不合規情況將
risks which are not deemed to be under acceptable limits. Any             加以識別並及時糾正。
non-compliance will also be identified and rectified on a timely
basis.

safe and stable working environment effectively.                          安全及穩定的工作環境。

the internal guidelines. The Group is pleased to report that the          團的意外及受傷比率極低，並無發生致命意外。

                                                                INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’S REPORT
                                                                                      獨立核數師報告


TO THE MEMBERS OF                                                    致匯聚科技有限公司股東
TIME INTERCONNECT TECHNOLOGY LIMITED
(incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability)          （於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公司）

OPINION                                                              意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Time        本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於 113
Interconnect Technology Limited (the “Company”) and its              至 207 頁的匯聚科技有限公司（以下簡稱「貴公
subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”) set out on    司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）的綜合
pages 113 to 207, which comprise the consolidated statement of       財務報表，此財務報表包括於二零一九年三月
financial position as at 31 March 2019, and the consolidated         三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該日止年度
statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income,          的綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權益變動
consolidated statement of changes in equity and consolidated         表 和 綜 合 現 金 流 量 表，以 及 綜 合 財 務 報 表 附
statement of cash flows for the year then ended, and notes to the    註，包括主要會計政策概要。
consolidated financial statements, including a summary of
significant accounting policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true    我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計
and fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group    師公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的《香港財務
as at 31 March 2019, and of its consolidated financial               報告準則》（「《香港財務報告準則》」）真實而中肯
performance and its consolidated cash flows for the year then        地反映了 貴集團於二零一九年三月三十一日
ended in accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting               的綜合財務狀況及截至該日止年度的綜合財務
Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of            表現及綜合現金流量，並已遵照香港《公司條
Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been properly       例》的披露要求妥為擬備。
prepared in compliance with the disclosure requirements of the
Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

BASIS FOR OPINION                                                    意見的基礎

      governance, we determine those matters that were of most             對本期綜合財務報表的審計最為重要，因而構

      of the current period and are therefore the key audit matters. We    些事項，除非法律法規不允許公開披露這些事
      describe these matters in our auditor’s report unless law or         項，或在極端罕見的情況下，如果合理預期在
      regulation precludes public disclosure about the matter or when,     我們報告中溝通某事項造成的負面後果超過產
      in extremely rare circumstances, we determine that a matter          生的公眾利益，我們決定不應在報告中溝通該
      should not be communicated in our report because the adverse         事項。
      consequences of doing so would reasonably be expected to
      outweigh the public interest benefits of such communication.

     interpretations.                                                       會計準則第 11 號「建築合約」及相關詮釋。

     cumulative effect of initially applying this standard                  15 號，而首次應用該準則的累計影響已於
     recognised at the date of initial application, 1 April 2018.           二零一八年四月一日首次應用當日確認。

     in the opening accumulated profits (or other components of             利（或適用的其他權益部分）確認，但並無

     been restated. Furthermore, in accordance with the                     報告準則第 15 號之過渡性條文，本集團
     transition provisions in HKFRS 15, the Group has elected to            僅選擇對二零一八年四月一日尚未完成之
     apply the standard retrospectively only to contracts that are          合約追溯應用該準則。因此，由於可資比
     not completed at 1 April 2018. Accordingly, certain                    較資料已根據香港會計準則第 18 號及香
     comparative information may not be comparable as                       港會計準則第 11 號及相關詮釋編製，故
     comparative information was prepared under HKAS 18 and                 若干可資比較資料未必可予比較。
     HKAS 11 and the related interpretations.

     The Group recognises revenue from sales of cable assembly              本集團自銷售電線組件產品確認收益。
     products.

           requirements relating to subleases and lease modifications.          括有關轉租及租賃修改的規定。

           Distinctions of operating leases and finance leases are              事項承租人會計處理中移除對經營租賃及
           removed for lessee accounting, and is replaced by a model            融資租賃的區分，並以承租人須就所有租
           where a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability have        賃確認使用權資產及相應負債的模式取

           under leases to which HKAS 17 applies. Based on the                   17 號適用的租賃權利及責任。根據香港財
           definition of lease payments under HKFRS 16, such deposits            務報告準則第 16 號的租賃付款定義，該
           are not payments relating to the right to use the underlying          等按金並非與使用相關資產的權利相關的

           may be adjusted to amortised cost. Adjustments to                     整為攤銷成本。調整已支付的可退還租賃
           refundable rental deposits paid would be considered as                按金將被視為額外租賃付款並計入使用權

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                   3.   主要會計政策
     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared               綜合財務報表乃按照歷史成本法及以下與
     on the historical cost basis and in accordance with HKFRSs             香港會計師公會頒佈之香港財務報告準則
     issued by the HKICPA. In addition, the consolidated financial          相符之會計政策編製。此外，綜合財務報
     statements include applicable disclosures required by the              表包括聯交所證券上市規則及香港公司條
     Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the Stock                 例規定之適用披露。
     Exchange and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

     Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the            歷史成本一般根據為交換貨品及服務而付
     consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                出代價之公平值計算。

     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset         公平值是於計量日市場參與者於有秩序交
     or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction               易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付之
     between market participants at the measurement date,                    價格，而不論該價格是否可直接觀察或使
     regardless of whether that price is directly observable or              用其他估值方法估計。於估計資產或負債
     estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating              之公平值時，本集團會考慮市場參與者於
     the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into         計量日對資產或負債定價時所考慮之資產
     account the characteristics of the asset or liability if the            或 負 債 特 點。 綜 合 財 務 報 表 中 作 計 量
     market participants would take those characteristics into               及╱或披露用途之公平值乃按此基準釐
     account when pricing the asset or liability at the                      定，惟於香港財務報告準則第 2 號「以股
     measurement date. Fair value for measurement and/or                     份為基礎付款」範圍內之以股份為基礎之
     disclosure purposes in the consolidated financial statements            付款交易、於香港會計準則第 17 號「租賃」
     is determined on such basis, except for share-based payment             範圍內之租賃交易及與公平值類似但並非
     transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS 2 “Share-               公平值之計量（如香港會計準則第 2 號「存
     based Payments”, leasing transactions that are within the               貨」之可變現淨值或香港會計準則第 36 號
     scope of HKAS 17 “Leases”, and measurements that have                  「資產減值」所用之使用價值）除外。
     some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as
     net realisable value in HKAS 2 “Inventories” or value in use
     in HKAS 36 “Impairment of Assets”.

           In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value                此外，就財務報告而言，公平值計量根據
           measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on               公平值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度及公平
           the degree to which the inputs to the fair value                         值計量之輸入數據對其整體重要性分類為
           measurements are observable and the significance of the                  第一、第二或第三級，詳情如下：
           inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety, which
           are described as follows:

           •     Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active            •   第一級輸入數據為實體有能力於計
                 markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity            量日評估的完全相同之資產或負債
                 can access at the measurement date;                                    於活躍市場之報價（未經調整）；

           •     Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices                •   第二級輸入數據為不包括第一級報
                 included within Level 1, that are observable for the                   價的資產或負債之可直接或間接觀
                 asset or liability, either directly or indirectly; and                 察之輸入數據；及

           •     Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or            •   第三級輸入數據為資產或負債之不
                 liability.                                                             可觀察輸入數據。

           Basis of consolidation                                                   綜合基準
           The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                    綜合財務報表包括本公司及其所控制的實
           financial statements of the Company and entities controlled              體（其附屬公司）之財務報表。倘屬以下情
           by the Company (its subsidiaries). Control is achieved when              況，則本公司獲得控制權：
           the Company:

           •     has power over the investee;                                       •   對被投資方擁有權力；

           •     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its            •   通過對被投資方之參與面臨可變回
                 involvement with the investee; and                                     報 之 風 險 或 取 得 可 變 回 報 之 權 利；
                                                                                        及

           •     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.            •   有能力行使其權力以影響其回報。

           The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee              倘有事實及情況顯示上列三項控制權條件
           if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to            之其中一項或多項有變，本集團會重新評
           one or more of the three elements of control listed above.               估其是否控制被投資方。

           bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of          品或服務）或一系列大致相同的特定商品
           distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.           或服務。

           over time by reference to the progress towards complete               間轉移，而收益則參照完全履行相關履約
           satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one of         責任的進展情況而隨時間確認：
           the following criteria is met:

           •     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes               •   本集團履約時，客戶同時取得並耗
                 the benefits provided by the Group’s performance as                 用本集團履約所提供的利益；
                 the Group performs;

                 that the customer controls as the Group performs; or                而該項資產於本集團履約時由客戶

                 with an alternative use to the Group and the Group                  替代用途的資產，且本集團對迄今
                 has an enforceable right to payment for performance                 已完成履約具有可強制執行的收取
                 completed to date.                                                  款項權利。

           Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when              否則，收益於客戶取得特指商品或服務之
           the customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.         控制權時確認。

           reliably measured; when it is probable that future economic            來經濟利益很有可能流入本集團時及當本

           described below.

           goods are delivered and titles have passed.                            轉移時確認。

           Service income is recognised when services are provided.               服務收入於提供服務時確認。

           Interest income is accrued on a time basis, by reference to            利息收入以時間基準參照未償還本金並按
           the principal outstanding and at the effective interest rate           適用的實際利率計算，實際利率乃將估計
           applicable, which is the rate that exactly discounts the               日後現金收入按金融資產的預期使用年期
           estimated future cash receipts through the expected life of            準確折現至該資產於初始確認時的賬面淨
           the financial asset to that asset’s net carrying amount on             額的利率。
           initial recognition.

           Property, plant and equipment                                          物業、廠房及設備
           Property, plant and equipment held for use in production of            持有用於提供生產服務或行政目的的物
           services, or for administrative purpose are stated in the              業、廠房及設備按成本減其後累計折舊及
           consolidated statement of financial position at cost less              累計減值虧損（如有）於綜合財務狀況表列
           subsequent accumulated depreciation and accumulated                    賬。
           impairment losses, if any.

           Depreciation is recognised so as to write off the cost of              折舊乃按直線法或餘額遞減法於其估計可
           items of assets less their residual values over their estimated        使用年期內確認，以撇銷該資產項目成本
           useful lives, using the straight-line method or reducing               減其剩餘價值。於各報告期末檢討估計可
           balance method. The estimated useful lives, residual values            使用年期、剩餘價值及折舊方法，而任何
           and depreciation method are reviewed at the end of each                估計變動的影響按預期基準入賬。
           reporting period, with the effect of any changes in estimate
           accounted for on a prospective basis.

           An item of property, plant and equipment is derecognised               物業、廠房及設備項目會在出售或預期繼
           upon disposal or when no future economic benefits are                  續使用資產不會帶來未來經濟利益時解除
           expected to arise from the continued use of the asset. Any             確認。物業、廠房及設備項目在出售或報
           gain or loss arising on the disposal or retirement of an item          廢時產生的任何收益或虧損，乃釐定為出
           of property, plant and equipment is determined as the                  售所得款項與有關資產賬面值的差額，並
           difference between the sales proceeds and the carrying                 於損益中確認。
           amount of the asset and is recognised in profit or loss.

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                      3.   主要會計政策（續）
     Leasing                                                               租賃
     Leases are classified as finance leases whenever the terms of         倘租賃條款將擁有權絕大部分風險及回報
     the lease transfer substantially all the risks and rewards of         轉 讓 予 承 租 人，則 租 賃 被 分 類 為 融 資 租
     ownership to the lessee. All other leases are classified as           賃。所有其他租賃則分類為經營租賃。
     operating leases.

     The Group as lessee                                                   本集團作為承租人
     Operating lease payments are recognised as an expense on              經營租賃付款於租賃期按直線法確認為開
     a straight-line basis over the lease term.                            支。

     Leasehold land and building                                           租賃土地及樓宇
     When the Group makes payments for a property interest                 當本集團就同時包括租賃土地及樓宇部分
     which includes both leasehold land and building elements,             之物業權益付款時，本集團會根據各部分
     the Group assesses the classification of each element as a            所有權所附之絕大部分風險及回報是否已
     finance or an operating lease separately based on the                 轉移至本集團之評估，分別將各部分分類
     assessment as to whether substantially all the risks and              為融資或經營租賃，除非兩個部分毫無疑
     rewards incidental to ownership of each element have been             問為經營租賃，於該情況下整項物業將入
     transferred to the Group, unless it is clear that both                賬列為經營租賃。具體而言，全部代價（包
     elements are operating leases in which case the entire                括任何一次性預付款項）於初始確認時將
     property is accounted as an operating lease. Specifically, the        按租賃權益相關的公平值，按比例在土地
     entire consideration (including any lump-sum upfront                  部分與樓宇部分間分配。
     payments) are allocated between the leasehold land and the
     building elements in proportion to the relative fair values of
     the leasehold interests in the land element and building
     element of the lease at initial recognition.

     To the extent the allocation of the relevant payments can be          倘相關付款能可靠分配，則以經營租賃入
     made reliably, interest in leasehold land that is accounted           賬之租賃土地權益於綜合財務狀況表中呈
     for as an operating lease is presented as “prepaid lease              列為「預付租賃付款」，並於租期內以直線
     payments” in the consolidated statement of financial                  法攤銷。
     position and is amortised over the lease term on a straight-
     line basis.

     Government grants                                                     政府補助
     Government grants are not recognised until there is                   政府補助直至可合理確保本集團將遵守補
     reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the              助所附帶的條件及收取補助時方予以確認。
     conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be
     received.

     Government grants that are receivable as compensation for             政府補助乃作為已發生的支出或虧損補
     expenses or losses already incurred or for the purpose of             償、或是以給予本集團即時財務援助為目
     giving immediate financial support to the Group with no               的而發放，無未來相關成本，並在應收期
     future related costs are recognised in profit or loss in the          間內於損益中確認。
     period in which they become receivable.

     Inventories                                                              存貨
     Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable           存貨乃按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低
     value. Cost is calculated using the first-in, first-out method.          者 列 賬。存 貨 成 本 乃 採 用 先 進 先 出 法 釐
     Net realisable value represents the estimated selling price for          定。可變現淨值指存貨的估計售價減所有

     necessary to make the sale.

     Financial instruments                                                    金融工具
     Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when           金融資產及金融負債於集團實體成為工具
     a group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions             合約條文的訂約方時確認。所有日常買賣
     of the instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of                 之金融資產於交易日確認及取消確認。日
     financial assets are recognised and derecognised on a trade              常買賣指須根據市場規則或慣例訂立之時
     date basis. Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or              限內交收資產之金融資產買賣。
     sales of financial assets that require delivery of assets within
     the time frame established by regulation or convention in
     the marketplace.

     Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured        金融資產及金融負債最初按公平值計量，
     at fair value except for trade receivables arising from                  惟自二零一八年四月一日起初步根據香港
     contracts with customers which are initially measured in                 財務報告準則第 15 號計量之來自客戶合
     accordance with HKFRS 15 since 1 April 2018. Transaction                 約所產生之貿易應收款項除外。因收購或
     costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition or issue         發行金融資產及金融負債而直接產生的交
     of financial assets and financial liabilities are added to or            易成本於初始確認時於該項金融資產或金
     deducted from the fair value of the financial assets or                  融負債的公平值中計入或扣除（如適用）。
     financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition.

     The effective interest method is a method of calculating the             實際利息法乃計算金融資產或金融負債的
     amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of        攤銷成本以及相關期間利息收入及利息開
     allocating interest income and interest expense over the                 支分配的方法。實際利率乃於初始確認時
     relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate that            按金融資產或金融負債的預計年期或適用
     exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                     的較短期間準確折現估計未來現金收入及
     payments (including all fees and points paid or received that            付款（包括構成實際利率不可或缺部分的
     form an integral part of the effective interest rate,                    全部已付或已收費用、交易成本及其他溢
     transaction costs and other premiums or discounts) through               價或折讓）至賬面淨額的利率。
     the expected life of the financial asset or financial liability,
     or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying
     amount on initial recognition.

     (i)   Significant increase in credit risk (continued)                    (i) 信貸風險大幅增加（續）
           In particular, the following information is taken into                 尤其是，評估信貸風險有否大幅增
           account when assessing whether credit risk has                         加時會考慮下列資料：
           increased significantly:

          •     an actual or expected significant deterioration in              •   金融工具外界（如有）或內部信
                the financial instrument’s external (if available)                  貸評級的實際或預期重大惡
                or internal credit rating;                                          化；

          •     significant deterioration in external market                    •   信貸風險的外界市場指標的重
                indicators of credit risk, e.g. a significant increase              大惡化，例如信貸息差大幅增
                in the credit spread, the credit default swap                       加，債務人的信貸違約掉期價
                prices for the debtor;                                              大幅上升；

          •     existing or forecast adverse changes in business,               •   業務、財務或經濟狀況的現有
                financial or economic conditions that are                           或預測不利變動，預期將導致
                expected to cause a significant decrease in the                     債務人履行其債務責任的能力
                debtor’s ability to meet its debt obligations;                      大幅下降；

          •     an actual or expected significant deterioration in              •   債務人經營業績的實際或預期
                the operating results of the debtor;                                重大惡化；

          •     an actual or expected significant adverse change                •   債務人監管、經濟或技術環境
                in the regulatory, economic, or technological                       的實際或預期重大不利變動，
                environment of the debtor that results in a                         導致債務人履行其債務責任的
                significant decrease in the debtor’s ability to                     能力大幅下降。
                meet its debt obligations.

          Irrespective of the outcome of the above assessment,                  不論上述評估結果，本集團假定，
          the Group presumes that the credit risk has increased                 如合約付款逾期未付超過 30 天，則
          significantly since initial recognition when contractual              信貸風險自初始確認以來已大幅增
          payments are more than 30 days past due, unless the                   加，惟本集團擁有合理並有理據支
          Group has reasonable and supportable information                      持的資料顯示情況並非如此，則作
          that demonstrates otherwise.                                          別論。

          The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the                 本集團定期監察用以確定信貸風險
          criteria used to identify whether there has been a                    曾否顯著增加的標準的成效，並於
          significant increase in credit risk and revises them as               適當時候作出修訂，從而確保有關
          appropriate to ensure that the criteria are capable of                標準能夠於款項逾期前確定信貸風
          identifying significant increase in credit risk before the            險顯著增加。
          amount becomes past due.

                 including the Group, in full.                                             發生違約事件。

                   reasonable and supportable information to                                  較寬鬆的違約標準屬更合適則當別
                   demonstrate that a more lagging default criterion is                       論。
                   more appropriate.

           (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                                   (iii)   信貸減值的金融資產
                   A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more                      當發生一項或多項對金融資產估計
                   events of default that have a detrimental impact on                        未來現金流量有不利影響的違約事
                   the estimated future cash flows of that financial asset                    件時，金融資產出現信貸減值。金
                   have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is credit-                  融資產信貸減值的證據包括以下可
                   impaired includes observable data about the following                      觀察事件：
                   events:

                   (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the                (a)   發行人或借款人出現重大財政
                         borrower;                                                                  困難；

                   (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or past                      (b)   違約，例如拖賬或逾期事項；
                         due event;

                   (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                       (c)   借款人的放款人因與借款人出
                         contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                             現財務困難有關之經濟或合約
                         financial difficulty, having granted to the                                理由而給予借款人在其他情況
                         borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s)                                下放款人不予考慮之優惠條
                         would not otherwise consider;                                              件；

                         enter bankruptcy or other financial                                        財務重組；或

                   (e)   the disappearance of an active market for that                       (e)   由於財務困難致使金融資產的
                         financial asset because of financial difficulties.                         活躍市場消失。

           counterparty has been placed under liquidation or has                          項已逾期超過一年）（以較早發生者

           trade receivables, when the amounts are over one year                          考慮法律意見（如適用）後，已撇銷

           written off may still be subject to enforcement                                回程序下之執法活動。撇銷構成取
           activities under the Group’s recovery procedures,                              消確認事件。其後的任何收回均在

     (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                                        (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
           The measurement of ECL is a function of the                                     預期信貸虧損的計量依據為違約概
           probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                            率、違約損失率（即違約時的損失程
           magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and the                            度）及違約風險的函數。評估違約概
           exposure at default. The assessment of the probability                          率及違約損失率的依據是過往數據，
           of default and loss given default is based on historical                        並按前瞻性資料調整。預期信貸風
           data adjusted by forward-looking information.                                   險的估計反映不偏不倚及概率加權
           Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-                         金額，以各自發生違約的風險為權
           weighted amount that is determined with the                                     重釐定。
           respective risks of default occurring as the weights.

           Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                                一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據合
           contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                             約應付本集團的所有訂約現金流量
           accordance with the contract and the cash flows that                            與本集團預計收取的現金流量（按於
           the Group expects to receive, discounted at the                                 初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼現）之
           effective interest rate determined at initial recognition.                      間的差額。

           Where ECL is measured on a collective basis or cater                            倘預期信貸虧損按整體基準計量或
           for cases where evidence at the individual instrument                           迎合個別工具層面證據未必存在之
           le v e l m a y n o t y e t b e a va i l a b l e , the fi nanc i al              情況，則金融工具按以下基準歸類：
           instruments are grouped on the following basis:

           •      nature of financial instruments;                                         •   金融工具之性質

           Financial liabilities and equity instruments                          金融負債及權益工具
           Classification as debt or equity                                      分類為債務或權益
           Debt and equity instruments are classified as either financial        債務及權益工具乃根據合約安排的實際性
           liabilities or as equity in accordance with the substance of          質及金融負債與權益工具的定義而被分類

           Equity instruments                                                    權益工具
           An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a                 權益工具乃證明本集團的資產於扣除所有
           residual interest in the assets of the Group after deducting          負債後的剩餘權益的任何合約。集團實體
           all of its liabilities. Equity instruments issued by group            發行的權益工具於收到所得款項時予以確
           entities are recognised at the proceeds received, net of              認（扣除直接發行成本）。
           direct issue costs.

           Effective interest method                                             實際利息法
           The effective interest method is a method of calculating the          實際利息法乃計算金融負債的攤銷成本以
           amortised cost of a financial liability and of allocating             及相關期間利息開支分配的方法。實際利
           interest expense over the relevant period. The effective              率乃於初始確認時按金融負債的預計年期
           interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts estimated            或適用的較短期間準確折現估計未來現金
           future cash payments (including all fees and points paid or           付款（包括構成實際利率不可或缺部分的
           received that form an integral part of the effective interest         全部已付或已收費用及貼息、交易成本及
           rate, transaction costs and other premiums or discounts)              其他溢價或折扣）至賬面淨額的利率。
           through the expected life of the financial liability or, where
           appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on
           initial recognition.

           Interest expense is recognised on an effective interest basis.        利息開支按實際利息法確認。

     Financial guarantee contracts                                          財務擔保合約
     A financial guarantee contract is a contract that requires the         財務擔保合約乃規定發行人在當某債務人
     issuer to make specified payments to reimburse the holder              未能按照某債務工具之條款在到期時付款
     for a loss it incurs because a specified debtor fails to make          而出現虧損時向持有人作出具體償付之合
     payment when due in accordance with the terms of a debt                約。
     instrument.

     Financial guarantee contracts issued by the Group are                  本集團發行之財務擔保合約最初以其公平
     initially measured at their fair values and, if not designated         值 計 量，而 倘 非 指 定 為 按 公 平 值 計 入 損
     as at fair value through profit or loss, are subsequently              益，其後按以下列兩項之較高者計量：
     measured at the higher of:

     (i)    the amount of obligation under the contract, as                 (i)    根據香港財務報告準則第 9 號（自二
            determined in accordance with HKFRS 9 (since 1                         零一八年一月一日起）╱香港會計準
            January 2018)/HKAS 37 “Provisions, Contingent                          則 第 37 號「撥 備、或 然 負 債 及 或 然
            Liabilities and Contingent Assets” (before application                 資產」（於二零一八年一月一日應用
            of HKFRS 9 on 1 January 2018); and                                     香港財務報告準則第 9 號前）釐定合
                                                                                   約項下責任之金額；及

     (ii)   the amount initially recognised less, when appropriate,         (ii)   初始確認之金額減去於擔保期間確
            cumulative amortisation recognised over the guarantee                  認之累計攤銷（如適用）的金額。
            period.

     Derecognition                                                          取消確認
     The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the                 本集團僅於資產收取現金流量的合約權利
     contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire, or         屆滿時，或向另一實體轉讓金融資產及資
     when it transfers the financial asset and substantially all the        產擁有權絕大部分風險及回報時取消確認
     risks and rewards of ownership of the asset to another                 金融資產。
     entity.

     On derecognition of a financial asset at amortised cost, the           於取消確認按攤銷成本計量的金融資產
     difference between the asset’s carrying amount and the sum             時，資產賬面值與已收及應收代價的總和
     of the consideration received and receivable is recognised in          間的差額，會於損益確認。
     profit or loss.

     The Group derecognises financial liabilities when, and only            本集團只有在其責任獲免除、取消或屆滿
     when, the Group’s obligations are discharged, cancelled or             時，方會取消確認金融負債。獲取消確認
     have expired. The difference between the carrying amount               的金融負債的賬面值與已付及應付代價間
     of the financial liability derecognised and the consideration          的差額於損益確認。
     paid and payable is recognised in profit or loss.

           Impairment on tangible assets                                          有形資產減值
           At the end of the reporting period, the Group reviews the              於報告期末，本集團審閱其有形資產的賬
           carrying amounts of its tangible assets to determine whether           面值，以確定有否任何跡象顯示該等資產
           there is any indication that those assets have suffered an             蒙受減值虧損。倘存在任何有關跡象，則
           impairment loss. If any such indication exists, the recoverable        會估計有關資產的可收回金額，以決定減
           amount of the relevant asset is estimated in order to                  值虧損（如有）的程度。
           determine the extent of the impairment loss, if any.

           The recoverable amount of tangible assets are estimated                有形資產的可收回金額個別估計。倘不可
           individually. When it is not possible to estimate the                  能個別估計可收回金額，本集團估計該資
           recoverable amount individually, the Group estimates the               產所屬的現金產生單位的可收回金額。倘
           recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit to which                可識別合理一致的分配基準，公司資產亦
           the asset belongs. When a reasonable and consistent basis              會被分配至個別現金產生單位，否則會被
           of allocation can be identified, corporate assets are also             分配至可識別合理一致的分配基準的最小
           allocated to individual cash-generating units, or otherwise            現金產生單位組別中。
           they are allocated to the smallest group of cash-generating
           units for which a reasonable and consistent allocation basis
           can be identified.

           Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs of           可收回金額為公平值減出售成本與使用價
           disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the              值的較高者。評估使用價值時，估計未來
           estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present            現金流量按稅前折現率折現至其現值。該
           value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current              折現率反映現時市場對貨幣時間值及未調
           market assessments of the time value of money and the                  整未來現金流量估計的資產（或現金產生
           risks specific to the asset (or a cash-generated unit) for             單位）特定風險的評估。
           which the estimates of future cash flows have not been
           adjusted.

           If the recoverable amount of an asset is estimated to be less          倘估計資產的可收回金額低於其賬面值，
           than its carrying amount, the carrying amount of the asset             則資產（或現金產生單位）賬面值扣減至其
           (or a cash-generated unit) is reduced to its recoverable               可收回金額。減值虧損即時於損益確認。
           amount. An impairment loss is recognised immediately in
           profit or loss.

           Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the                    倘若減值虧損於其後撥回，資產（或現金
           carrying amount of the asset (or a cash-generated unit) is             產生單位）之賬面值將調升至其經修訂之
           increased to the revised estimate of its recoverable amount,           估計可收回金額，但增加後之賬面值不得
           but so that the increased carrying amount does not exceed              超過倘若該資產（或現金產生單位）在過往
           the carrying amount that would have been determined had                年度並無確認減值虧損時原應確認之賬面
           no impairment loss been recognised for the asset (or a cash-           值。減值虧損之撥回即時於損益內確認。
           generated unit) in prior years. A reversal of an impairment
           loss is recognised immediately in profit or loss.

     Taxation                                                                 稅項
     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently               所得稅開支指當期應付稅項及遞延稅項之
     payable and deferred tax.                                                總和。

     The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the             當期應付稅項乃根據年內的應課稅溢利計
     year. Taxable profit differs from profit before taxation as              量。應課稅溢利與綜合損益及其他全面收
     reported in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and             益表中呈報的除稅前溢利不同，乃由於不
     other comprehensive income because it excludes items of                  包括其他年度的應課稅收入或可扣稅支
     income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other                出，且 不 包 括 毋 須 課 稅 及 不 可 扣 稅 的 項
     years and it further excludes items that are never taxable or            目。本集團當期稅項負債以報告期末已實
     deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax is calculated          施或實質上已實施之稅率計算。
     using tax rates that have been enacted or substantively
     enacted by the end of the reporting period.

     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                      遞延稅項根據綜合財務報表中資產及負債
     between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the            的賬面值與其用於計量應課稅溢利的相應
     consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax              稅基之間的暫時差額而確認。遞延稅項負
     base used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax             債通常會就所有應課稅暫時差額確認。倘
     liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary           於可能有應課稅溢利可動用抵銷該等可扣
     differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for            稅的暫時差額時，則一般就所有可扣稅的
     all deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is            暫時差額確認遞延稅項資產。如暫時差額
     probable that taxable profits will be available against which            源自對應課稅溢利及會計溢利皆無影響交
     those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such             易中其他資產及負債的初始確認，該等資
     assets and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary               產及負債將不予確認。
     difference arises from the initial recognition of other assets
     and liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable
     profit nor the accounting profit.

     Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary            遞延稅項負債乃按於附屬公司之投資相關
     differences associated with investments in subsidiaries,                 之應課稅暫時差額而確認，惟當本集團可
     except where the Group is able to control the reversal of the            控制暫時差額之撥回及暫時差額可能不會
     temporary difference and it is probable that the temporary               在可見將來撥回則除外。與該等投資有關
     difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future.                   可扣稅暫時差額產生之遞延稅項資產僅於
     Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary                    可能將有足夠應課稅溢利可動用暫時差額
     differences associated with such investments are only                    之利益，且彼等預期於可預見將來撥回之
     recognised to the extent that it is probable that there will be          情況方予以確認。
     sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits
     of the temporary differences and they are expected to
     reverse in the foreseeable future.

     The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at                遞延稅項資產的賬面值於各報告期末進行
     the end of each reporting period and reduced to the extent               審閱，並調減至不再可能具備足夠的應課
     that it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits            稅溢利以收回該項資產的全部或任何部分
     will be available to allow all or part of the asset to be                為止。
     recovered.

           liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate        根據於償還負債或變現資產期間預期適用
           (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively                  的稅率計量。
           enacted by the end of the reporting period.

           The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                  遞延稅項負債及資產的計量反映倘按本集
           reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                團預期於報告期末收回或結算其資產及負
           manner in which the Group expects, at the end of the                    債賬面值計算的稅項後果。
           reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount of
           its assets and liabilities.

           Current and deferred tax is recognised in profit or loss,               當期及遞延稅項於損益確認，惟在其與於
           except when they relate to items that are recognised in                 其他全面收益或直接於權益內確認的項目
           other comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which              有關的情況下，當期及遞延稅項亦會分別
           case, the current and deferred tax are also recognised in               於其他全面收益或直接於權益內確認。
           other comprehensive income or directly in equity
           respectively.

      4.   KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY                              4.   估計不明朗因素之主要來源及關鍵
           AND CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENT                                       會計判斷
           In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which            於應用本集團之會計政策（於附註 3 陳述）
           are described in note 3, the directors of the Company are               時，本公司董事需對未能透過其他來源直
           required to make judgements, estimates and assumptions                  接確定之資產及負債之賬面值作出判斷、
           about the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities that are           估計及假設。該等估計及相關假設乃基於
           not readily apparent from other sources. The estimates and              過往經驗及其他被視為相關之因素。實際
           associated assumptions are based on historical experience               結果可能有別於該等估計。
           and other factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual
           results may differ from these estimates.

           The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on                估計及相關假設乃按持續基準被審閱。如
           an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                對會計估計之修訂僅影響進行修訂之期
           recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if            間，則於修訂估計的期間確認修訂，或如
           the revision affects only that period, or in the period of the          修訂同時影響目前及未來期間，則於修訂
           revision and future periods if the revision affects both                及未來期間確認。
           current and future periods.

           Critical judgement in applying accounting policies                      應用會計政策的關鍵判斷
           The following is the critical judgement, apart from those               以下為董事於應用本集團會計政策過程中

          The Group allows an average credit period of ranging from         本集團給予其貿易客戶之平均信貸期介乎
          30 days to 120 days to its trade customers. Before accepting      30 日至 120 日。在接納任何新客戶之前，
          any new customers, the Group will internally assess the           本集團將對潛在客戶之信貸質素作內部評
          credit quality of the potential customers and define              估，並釐訂合適信貸限額。貿易應收款項

25. SHARE-BASED PAYMENT TRANSACTION                                  25. 以股份付款的交易
   The Company’s share option scheme was adopted pursuant              本公司的購股權計劃（「購股權計劃」）已根
   to a resolution passed on 24 January 2018 (the “Share               據二零一八年一月二十四日通過的決議案
   Option Scheme”).                                                    獲採用。

   The purpose of the Share Option Scheme is to attract and            購股權計劃旨在吸引及挽留最稱職人員、
   retain the best available personnel, to provide additional          向本集團僱員（全職及兼職）、董事、諮詢
   incentive to eligible participant(s) including employees (full-     人、顧問、分銷商、承包商、供應商、代
   time and part-time), directors, consultants, advisers,              理、客戶、商業夥伴或服務供應商提供額
   distributors, contractors, suppliers, agents, customers,            外獎勵以及推動本集團業務創出佳績。
   business partners or service providers of the Group and to
   promote the success of the business of the Group.

   The maximum entitlement of each eligible participant under          根據購股權計劃，每名合資格參與者可獲
   the Share Option Scheme should be (1) not to exceed 1% of           授之最高數目 (1) 不應超過任何十二個月
   the shares in issue in any 12-month period unless approved          期間已發行股份的 1%（除非經本公司股
   by the shareholders of the Company; and (2) Options                 東批准） ；及 (2) 倘於任何一年內授予主要
   granted to substantial shareholder or independent non-              股東或獨立非執行董事或彼等各自的聯繫
   executive directors or their respective associates in any one       人的購股權超過已發行股份的 0.1% 且價
   year exceeding 0.1% of the Shares in issue and with a value         值超過 5,000,000 港元，則須經本公司股
   in excess of HK$5,000,000 must be approved by the                   東批准。
   shareholders of the Company.

   Options granted must be taken up within one month of the            所授購股權須於授出日期起計一個月內獲
   date of grant, upon payment of HK$1 on acceptance of the            接納，於接納要約時須支付 1 港元。自購
   offer. Options may be exercised at any time during a period         股權獲接納及授出當日或其後日期至董事
   commencing on or after the date on which the option is              會知會各承授人的到期日期間內，購股權
   accepted and granted and expiring on a date to be notified          可獲行使，該期間不應超過購股權獲接納
   by the Board of Directors to each grantee which shall not be        及授出的日期起計十年。
   more than 10 years from the date on which the option is
   accepted and granted.

   The Group operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme               本集團為香港的所有合資格僱員參與強制

   cap of monthly relevant income of HK$30,000 of                     入息之 30,000 港元，有關供款乃與僱員的

   The Group also makes voluntary contribution in addition to         出自願供款。
   the mandatory contribution.

34. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           34. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the         為確保本集團的實體將可持續經營，本集
   Group will be able to continue as a going concern while              團對其資本進行管理，並透過使債務及權
   maximising the return to stakeholders through the                    益達致最佳平衡為股東締造最大回報。本
   optimisation of the debt and equity balances. The Group’s            集團的整體策略於整個年度內維持不變。
   overall strategy remains unchanged throughout the year.

   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debts,            本集團的資本架構包括債務淨額（包括附
